  const modelContext = navigator.modelContext;
  if (!modelContext) {
    return;
  }

  const toolDefinitions = [{"kind":"docs-search","name":"render.docs.search","description":"Search Render documentation by keyword.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"query":{"type":"string","description":"Keywords to search for in the Render docs."}},"required":["query"],"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"docs-markdown","name":"render.docs.get-markdown","description":"Fetch a Render docs page as markdown by slug.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{"slug":{"type":"string","description":"Docs page slug without a leading slash. Nested slugs are allowed."}},"required":["slug"],"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"llms-index","name":"render.llms.get-index","description":"Fetch the Render llms.txt index as markdown.","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"blog-index","name":"render.blog.get-index","description":"Fetch the latest Render blog index as markdown (most recent 20 posts).","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}},{"kind":"articles-index","name":"render.articles.get-index","description":"Fetch the latest Render articles index as markdown (most recent 50 articles).","inputSchema":{"type":"object","properties":{},"additionalProperties":false},"annotations":{"readOnlyHint":true}}];

  const buildStructuredResult = (value) => ({
    content: [
      {
        type: 'text',
        text: typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value, null, 2),
      },
    ],
    structuredContent: typeof value === 'string' ? { text: value } : value,
  });

  const normalizeSlug = (value) =>
    String(value ?? '')
      .trim()
      .replace(/^\/+/, '')
      .replace(/\.md$/i, '');

  const encodeSlug = (slug) =>
    slug
      .split('/')
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((segment) => encodeURIComponent(segment))
      .join('/');

  const fetchJson = async (path) => {
    const response = await fetch(path, {
      headers: {
        Accept: 'application/json',
      },
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Request failed with status ${response.status} for ${path}`);
    }

    return response.json();
  };

  const fetchText = async (path) => {
    const response = await fetch(path, {
      headers: {
        Accept: 'text/markdown, text/plain;q=0.9',
      },
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Request failed with status ${response.status} for ${path}`);
    }

    return response.text();
  };

  const tools = toolDefinitions.map((tool) => ({
    name: tool.name,
    description: tool.description,
    inputSchema: tool.inputSchema,
    annotations: tool.annotations,
    execute: async (input) => {
      switch (tool.kind) {
        case 'docs-search': {
          const query = String(input?.query ?? '').trim();
          if (!query) {
            throw new Error('The "query" field is required.');
          }

          const result = await fetchJson('/api/agent/docs-search?query=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
          return buildStructuredResult(result);
        }
        case 'docs-markdown': {
          const slug = normalizeSlug(input?.slug);
          if (!slug) {
            throw new Error('The "slug" field is required.');
          }

          const markdown = await fetchText('/docs/' + encodeSlug(slug) + '.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            slug,
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'llms-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/llms.txt');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/llms.txt',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'blog-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/blog.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/blog.md',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        case 'articles-index': {
          const markdown = await fetchText('/articles.md');
          return buildStructuredResult({
            path: '/articles.md',
            markdown,
          });
        }
        default: {
          throw new Error('Unsupported WebMCP tool.');
        }
      }
    },
  }));

  if (typeof modelContext.registerTool === 'function') {
    for (const tool of tools) {
      try {
        modelContext.registerTool(tool);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to register WebMCP tool', tool.name, error);
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  if (typeof modelContext.provideContext === 'function') {
    modelContext.provideContext({ tools });
  }

dark:text-purple-100">
<p><strong>Migrating a large database from Heroku?</strong></p>
<p>The Render team can help you orchestrate your migration to minimize downtime, even for apps with multi-terabyte databases.</p>
<p><a rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://render.com/migrate-from-heroku"><span class="ease transition-colors group relative z-[1] flex cursor-pointer items-center overflow-hidden font-montreal whitespace-nowrap justify-between motion-safe:duration-150 motion-reduce:duration-0 lg:motion-safe:duration-300 lg:motion-reduce:duration-0 bg-background--inverted text-text-primary--inverted dark:lg:hover:text-white dark:lg:group-hover:text-white dark:active:text-white dark:focus-visible:text-white h-[48px] text-body-sm-new px-24 gap-10 lg:[--button-arrow-offset:2px] not-prose !inline-flex !text-[16px]"><span class="ease pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 z-[0] block h-full w-full opacity-0 transition-opacity group-focus-visible:opacity-100 group-active:opacity-100 origin-right lg:scale-x-0 lg:opacity-100 lg:transition-transform lg:group-active:origin-left lg:group-active:scale-x-100 lg:group-focus-visible:origin-left lg:group-focus-visible:scale-x-100 lg:group-hover:origin-left lg:group-hover:scale-x-100 motion-safe:duration-150 motion-reduce:duration-0 lg:motion-safe:duration-300 lg:motion-reduce:duration-0 bg-purple-600"></span><span class="ease relative z-[1] inline-block motion-safe:duration-150 motion-reduce:duration-0 lg:motion-safe:duration-300 lg:motion-reduce:duration-0 translate-x-0 lg:transition-transform group-hover:motion-safe:translate-x-[--button-arrow-offset]">Contact the Render team</span><svg class="ease relative z-[1] translate-x-0 lg:transition-transform group-hover:motion-safe:-translate-x-[--button-arrow-offset] motion-safe:duration-150 motion-reduce:duration-0 lg:motion-safe:duration-300 lg:motion-reduce:duration-0 -mr-4 rotate-0 transform" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="10" height="20" viewBox="0 0 10 20" fill="currentColor"><rect x="6" y="9" width="2" height="2"></rect><rect x="4" y="7" width="2" height="2"></rect><rect x="2" y="5" width="2" height="2"></rect><rect x="4" y="11" width="2" height="2"></rect><rect x="2" y="13" width="2" height="2"></rect></svg></span></a></p>
</span>
<p>Render Postgres databases provide fully managed, scalable storage of relational data.</p>
<p>All paid Render Postgres databases provide <a href="/docs/postgresql-backups">point-in-time recovery</a> and on-demand logical exports. Larger instances support <a href="/docs/postgresql-read-replicas">read replicas</a> and <a href="/docs/postgresql-high-availability">high availability</a> for improved performance and reliability.</p>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="quickstarts"><a href="#quickstarts" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Quickstarts</h2>
<p>Here are a few Render quickstarts that include a Render Postgres database as part of their application stack:</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="/docs/deploy-django">Django</a></li>
<li><a href="/docs/deploy-rails-8">Rails</a></li>
<li><a href="/docs/deploy-phoenix-distillery">Phoenix</a></li>
</ul>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="create-your-database"><a href="#create-your-database" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Create your database</h2>
<div dir="ltr" data-orientation="horizontal" class="tab-switcher my-20" data-tab-switcher="true"><div role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="flex flex-wrap bg-gray-25 px-24 dark:bg-gray-800 sticky top-[--nav-bar-height] z-10 border dark:border-gray-700" tabindex="-1" data-orientation="horizontal" style="outline:none"><button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="true" aria-controls="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" data-state="active" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" class="group relative px-12 py-8 font-medium text-gray-600 text-sm transition-colors [--underline-height:0px] data-[state=active]:font-semibold data-[state=active]:text-black hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-black dark:text-gray-200 dark:data-[state=active]:text-white dark:hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-white data-[state=active]:[--underline-height:2px]" tabindex="-1" data-orientation="horizontal" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="invisible block h-0 font-semibold">Dashboard</span>Dashboard<div class="ease absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[--underline-height] motion-safe:transition-[height] bg-purple-600"></div></button><button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-cli" data-state="inactive" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" class="group relative px-12 py-8 font-medium text-gray-600 text-sm transition-colors [--underline-height:0px] data-[state=active]:font-semibold data-[state=active]:text-black hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-black dark:text-gray-200 dark:data-[state=active]:text-white dark:hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-white data-[state=active]:[--underline-height:2px]" tabindex="-1" data-orientation="horizontal" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="invisible block h-0 font-semibold">CLI</span>CLI<div class="ease absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[--underline-height] motion-safe:transition-[height] bg-purple-600"></div></button><button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-api" data-state="inactive" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-api" class="group relative px-12 py-8 font-medium text-gray-600 text-sm transition-colors [--underline-height:0px] data-[state=active]:font-semibold data-[state=active]:text-black hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-black dark:text-gray-200 dark:data-[state=active]:text-white dark:hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-white data-[state=active]:[--underline-height:2px]" tabindex="-1" data-orientation="horizontal" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="invisible block h-0 font-semibold">API</span>API<div class="ease absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[--underline-height] motion-safe:transition-[height] bg-purple-600"></div></button></div><div class="flex min-h-[4rem] flex-col border-t-0 p-24 border dark:border-gray-700">
<div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">
<ol>
<li>
<p>Go to <a href="https://dashboard.render.com/new/database" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">dashboard.render.com/new/database</a>, or click <strong>+ New &gt; Postgres</strong> in the Render Dashboard.</p>
<p>This form appears:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:500px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/8984d56d0ad0bd5e189fd7701ae582805f98ce28a9a713ec9dea4a9856ca1371/new-postgresql.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/8984d56d0ad0bd5e189fd7701ae582805f98ce28a9a713ec9dea4a9856ca1371/new-postgresql.png" width="1084" height="1640" alt="New Postgres creation form"/></picture></p>
</span>
</li>
<li>
<p>Provide a helpful <strong>Name</strong> for your database.</p>
<ul>
<li>You can change this value at any time.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Optionally fill in the <strong>Database</strong> and/or <strong>User</strong> fields if you want to set your PostgreSQL <code class="language-text">dbname</code> and/or username.</p>
<ul>
<li>Render generates values for either of these that you don&#x27;t specify.</li>
<li>You <em>can&#x27;t</em> change these values after creating your database.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Choose a <strong><span class="cursor-help whitespace-nowrap text-gray-600 underline decoration-1 decoration-dashed underline-offset-[3px] hover:text-gray-700 focus:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none dark:text-gray-100 dark:focus:text-white dark:hover:text-white" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Show definition for region" aria-expanded="false">Region</span></strong> to run your database in.</p>
<ul>
<li>Choose the same region as your services that will connect to the database. This minimizes latency and enables communication over your <span class="cursor-help whitespace-nowrap text-gray-600 underline decoration-1 decoration-dashed underline-offset-[3px] hover:text-gray-700 focus:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none dark:text-gray-100 dark:focus:text-white dark:hover:text-white" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Show definition for private network" aria-expanded="false">private network</span>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Optionally change the <strong>PostgreSQL Version</strong> if you want to use an older version.</p>
<ul>
<li>Major versions <!-- -->13<!-- --> through <!-- -->18<!-- --> are available for all new instances.</li>
<li>Versions 11 and 12 are available for workspaces that have at least one <em>existing</em> database on the corresponding version.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Scroll down and select a <strong>compute plan</strong> for your database. This determines its available CPU and RAM.</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p><a href="/docs/free#free-postgres">Learn about limitations of the Free compute plan</a>.</p>
</span>
<span class="block" style="max-width:550px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/f7949a1ceca31bb8e8fec14773ac79da5f70c6087be7b0edea8f9b6361315f33/pg-instance-types.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/f7949a1ceca31bb8e8fec14773ac79da5f70c6087be7b0edea8f9b6361315f33/pg-instance-types.png" width="1210" height="944" alt="Selecting a Postgres compute plan"/></picture></p>
</span>
<p>You can <a href="#changing-your-compute-plan">change your compute plan</a> later.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Scroll down and set your database&#x27;s initial storage, in GB.</p>
<ul>
<li>You can specify 1 GB or any multiple of 5 GB.</li>
<li>You can increase your storage later, but you can&#x27;t decrease it.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Optionally enable <strong>Storage Autoscaling</strong>.</p>
<ul>
<li>Whenever your database is 90% full, Render automatically increases its storage by 50%, rounded up to the nearest multiple of 5 GB. You can&#x27;t reduce storage after increasing it. <a href="#storage-autoscaling">Learn more</a>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Click <strong>Create Database</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>You&#x27;re all set! Your new database&#x27;s status updates to <strong>Available</strong> in the Render Dashboard when it&#x27;s ready to use.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="1-install-the-cli"><a href="#1-install-the-cli" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>1. Install the CLI</h4>

</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_398filubspiuidb_-trigger-direct-download" id="radix-_R_398filubspiuidb_-content-direct-download" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="direct-download">
<h5 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 font-montreal font-bold relative group/header" id="direct-download"><a href="#direct-download" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Direct download</h5>
<ol>
<li>Open the CLI&#x27;s <a href="https://github.com/render-oss/cli/releases/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">GitHub releases page</a>.</li>
<li>Download the executable that corresponds to your system&#x27;s architecture.</li>
</ol>
<p>If you use an architecture besides those provided, you can build from source instead.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_398filubspiuidb_-trigger-build-from-source" id="radix-_R_398filubspiuidb_-content-build-from-source" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="build-from-source">
<h5 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 font-montreal font-bold relative group/header" id="building-from-source"><a href="#building-from-source" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Building from source</h5>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p>We recommend building from source only if no other installation method works for your system.</p>
</span>
<ol>
<li>
<p><a href="https://golang.org/doc/install" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Install the Go programming language</a> if you haven&#x27;t already.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Clone and build the CLI project with the following commands:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:3"></template><!--/$-->
</li>
</ol>
</div>
</div></div>
<p>After installation completes, open a new terminal tab and run <code class="language-text">render</code> with no arguments to confirm.</p>
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="2-create-your-database"><a href="#2-create-your-database" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>2. Create your database</h4>
<p>Run <code class="language-text">render pg create</code> to walk through an interactive wizard:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:4"></template><!--/$-->
<p>To skip the wizard, provide flags directly. For example:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:5"></template><!--/$-->
<p>Supply the following options to configure your database:</p>
<div class="scrollable"><table class="docs-table my-30 docs-table-apiref"><thead><tr><th>Option</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--name</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>A name for your database. You can change this value at any time.</p>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--database-name</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Optionally set the PostgreSQL <code class="language-text">dbname</code>. Render generates a value if you don&#x27;t specify one.</p>
<p>You <em>can&#x27;t</em> change this value after creating your database.</p>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--database-user</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Optionally set the PostgreSQL username. Render generates a value if you don&#x27;t specify one.</p>
<p>You <em>can&#x27;t</em> change this value after creating your database.</p>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--region</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>The <span class="cursor-help whitespace-nowrap text-gray-600 underline decoration-1 decoration-dashed underline-offset-[3px] hover:text-gray-700 focus:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none dark:text-gray-100 dark:focus:text-white dark:hover:text-white" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Show definition for region" aria-expanded="false">region</span> to create the database in.</p>
<p>Choose the same region as your connecting services to minimize latency and enable communication over your <span class="cursor-help whitespace-nowrap text-gray-600 underline decoration-1 decoration-dashed underline-offset-[3px] hover:text-gray-700 focus:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none dark:text-gray-100 dark:focus:text-white dark:hover:text-white" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Show definition for private network" aria-expanded="false">private network</span>.</p>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--version</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Optionally specify a PostgreSQL version. Defaults to the latest supported version (<!-- -->18<!-- -->).</p>
<ul>
<li>Major versions <!-- -->13<!-- --> through <!-- -->18<!-- --> are available for all new instances.</li>
<li>Versions 11 and 12 are available for workspaces that have at least one <em>existing</em> database on the corresponding version.</li>
</ul>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--plan</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Specify a <a href="/docs/compute-plans?tab=postgres#all-plans">compute plan</a> to determine your database&#x27;s available RAM, CPU, and connection limit.</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p><a href="/docs/free#free-postgres">Learn about Free compute plan limitations</a>.</p>
</span>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--disk-size-gb</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Set your database&#x27;s initial storage in GB.</p>
<ul>
<li>You can specify 1 GB or any multiple of 5 GB.</li>
<li>You can increase your storage later, but you <em>can&#x27;t</em> decrease it.</li>
</ul>
</td></tr><tr><td>
<p><code class="language-text">--disk-autoscaling</code></p>
</td><td>
<p>Optionally enable <a href="#storage-autoscaling">storage autoscaling</a>.</p>
</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>For the full list of options, see the <a href="/docs/cli-reference#pg-create"><code class="language-text">pg create</code> reference</a>.</p>
<p>You&#x27;re all set! After successfully running the command, the Render CLI returns your new database&#x27;s details.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-trigger-api" id="radix-_R_filubspiuidb_-content-api" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="api">
<p>Using the <a href="/docs/api">Render API</a>, make a request to the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/create-postgres" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Create Postgres instance</a> endpoint.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="connect-to-your-database"><a href="#connect-to-your-database" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Connect to your database</h2>
<p>Every Render Postgres database has two different URLs for incoming connections:</p>
<ul>
<li>An <strong>internal URL</strong> for connections from your other Render services hosted in the <em>same region</em></li>
<li>An <strong>external URL</strong> for connections from <em>everything else</em></li>
</ul>
<!--$?--><template id="B:6"></template><!--/$-->
<p><strong>Use the internal URL wherever possible.</strong> It minimizes query latency by enabling communication over your <span class="cursor-help whitespace-nowrap text-gray-600 underline decoration-1 decoration-dashed underline-offset-[3px] hover:text-gray-700 focus:text-gray-700 focus:outline-none dark:text-gray-100 dark:focus:text-white dark:hover:text-white" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Show definition for private network" aria-expanded="false">private network</span>.</p>

<div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">
<p>Both URLs are available from the <strong>Connect</strong> menu in the top-right corner of your database&#x27;s page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:450px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/dfac8486ff2fe36ebe7033f2515b256e04398bae6f71b97c5a6c7298d38ea544/pg-connect-menu.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/dfac8486ff2fe36ebe7033f2515b256e04398bae6f71b97c5a6c7298d38ea544/pg-connect-menu.png" width="980" height="582" alt="The Connect menu for a Render Postgres database"/></picture></p>

<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">

<p>For more options, see the <a href="/docs/cli-reference#pg-get"><code class="language-text">pg get</code> reference</a>.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-trigger-api" id="radix-_R_rilubspiuidb_-content-api" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="api">
<p>Use the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/retrieve-postgres-connection-info" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Retrieve Postgres connection info</a> API endpoint to retrieve your database&#x27;s connection details.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<p>How you connect to your database depends on your code: some frameworks expect a single connection string or URL in an environment variable, while others need multiple connection parameters in a configuration file. See <a href="#quickstarts">Quickstarts</a> for examples.</p>
<p>At a minimum, your app needs to know your database&#x27;s hostname, port, username, password, and database name (such as <code class="language-text">mydb</code> in the <a href="https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/tutorial-createdb.html" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">official PostgreSQL tutorial</a>).</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p>Render Postgres uses the default PostgreSQL port <code class="language-text">5432</code>. You can usually leave this port unspecified.</p>
</span>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="internal-connections"><a href="#internal-connections" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Internal connections</h3>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p>To use the internal URL, your connecting service and your database must belong to the same account and <a href="/docs/regions">region</a>.</p>
</span>
<p>Wherever possible, connect to your database using its internal URL. Internal connection details are available on your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:600px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/0fca9443e5c6da5d9ae33b770fa6f65070f279e37ecee0b4b3ecbfdb7a8ed87b/postgresql-connection-details.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/0fca9443e5c6da5d9ae33b770fa6f65070f279e37ecee0b4b3ecbfdb7a8ed87b/postgresql-connection-details.png" width="1334" height="1096" alt="PostgreSQL private connection details in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
<p>You can view both individual details and the assembled internal URL, which has the following format:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:8"></template><!--/$-->
<p>Use whichever format your framework expects for database credentials.</p>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="external-connections"><a href="#external-connections" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>External connections</h3>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p><strong>External URL connections are slower because they traverse the public internet.</strong></p>
<p>To minimize latency, use your database&#x27;s <a href="#internal-connections">internal URL</a> when connecting from a Render service running in the same region.</p>
</span>
<p>Tools and systems outside of Render can connect to your database via its external URL, available from its <strong>Info</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:600px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/f0ccbf4aaa8a57f18fb0c4b8095d9929783b72f6276221a708de27f1563b5fbf/postgresql-external-connections.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/f0ccbf4aaa8a57f18fb0c4b8095d9929783b72f6276221a708de27f1563b5fbf/postgresql-external-connections.png" width="1320" height="310" alt="PostgreSQL public connection details in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
<p>Most database clients understand the external URL, which has the following format:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:9"></template><!--/$-->
<p>You can also run the provided <strong>PSQL Command</strong> directly in your terminal to start a psql session.</p>
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="common-external-connection-issues"><a href="#common-external-connection-issues" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Common external connection issues</h4>
<div class="mt-20 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-600"><div class="bg-gray-25 py-5 dark:bg-gray-700"><h6 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] !m-0 px-10 font-medium font-montreal text-black text-lg dark:text-white mb-10 mt-40 font-montreal relative group/header" id="fatal-no-sni-information-found"><a href="#fatal-no-sni-information-found" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>&quot;FATAL:  No SNI information found&quot;</h6></div><div class="p-20 [--ordered-list-item-top:0px] [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;*]:text-base [&amp;_p]:mt-15 [&amp;_p]:leading-[1.4] [&amp;_ul_li]:mt-5 [&amp;_ul_li]:mb-0">
<p>Make sure your PostgreSQL client always connects using your database&#x27;s full external connection URL, <em>not</em> an IP address that the hostname resolved to during a previous connection attempt.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="mt-20 border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-600"><div class="bg-gray-25 py-5 dark:bg-gray-700"><h6 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] !m-0 px-10 font-medium font-montreal text-black text-lg dark:text-white mb-10 mt-40 font-montreal relative group/header" id="tls-handshake-errors"><a href="#tls-handshake-errors" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>TLS handshake errors</h6></div><div class="p-20 [--ordered-list-item-top:0px] [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 [&amp;&gt;*]:text-base [&amp;_p]:mt-15 [&amp;_p]:leading-[1.4] [&amp;_ul_li]:mt-5 [&amp;_ul_li]:mb-0">
<p>Confirm that your PostgreSQL client supports TLS version 1.2 or higher, and that it supports any of the following cipher suites:</p>
<details>
<summary><strong>Click to show</strong></summary>
<ul>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256</code></li>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_AES_256_GCM_SHA384</code></li>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256</code></li>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_AES_128_GCM_SHA256</code></li>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256</code></li>
<li><code class="language-text">TLS_ECDHE_RSA_WITH_AES_256_GCM_SHA384</code></li>
</ul>
</details>
</div></div>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="restricting-external-access"><a href="#restricting-external-access" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Restricting external access</h3>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p><strong>These rules apply only to connections that use your database&#x27;s <a href="#external-connections"><strong>external URL</strong></a>.</strong></p>
<p>Your Render services in the same region as your database can always connect using your database&#x27;s <a href="#internal-connections">internal URL</a>.</p>
</span>
<p>By default, your Render Postgres instance is accessible from any IP address (if the connection uses valid credentials). You can modify this default behavior by restricting access to a set of IPs or even disabling external access entirely.</p>
<div dir="ltr" data-orientation="horizontal" class="tab-switcher my-20" data-tab-switcher="true"><div role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="flex flex-wrap bg-gray-25 px-24 font-semibold">API</span>API<div class="ease absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-[--underline-height] motion-safe:transition-[height] bg-purple-600"></div></button></div><div class="flex min-h-[4rem] flex-col border-t-0 p-24 border dark:border-gray-700">
<div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">
<p>In the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>, go to your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page and scroll down to the <strong>Networking</strong> section:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:500px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/dd1a7ef4b9d2b9f0d6ea885c5913b66b10d66c6ca0b36a6f0e2afffab8c27a5e/postgresql-access-control.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/dd1a7ef4b9d2b9f0d6ea885c5913b66b10d66c6ca0b36a6f0e2afffab8c27a5e/postgresql-access-control.png" width="1124" height="1130" alt="Setting PostgreSQL access control in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
<p>You can specify IP address blocks using <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classless_Inter-Domain_Routing#CIDR_blocks" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">CIDR notation</a>. The default block is <code class="language-text">0.0.0.0/0</code>, which allows access from any IP address.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">
<p>Set your database&#x27;s IP allow list using the <code class="language-text">render pg update</code> command with the <code class="language-text">--ip-allow-list</code> flag:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:a"></template><!--/$-->
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-warning.svg)] border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-800 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-200">
<p><strong>Passing <code class="language-text">--ip-allow-list</code> to this command <em>replaces</em> your database&#x27;s existing IP allow list.</strong></p>
<p>Make sure to include <em>all</em> address blocks you want to allow, including any existing blocks.</p>
</span>
<ul>
<li>Repeat the flag for each address block you want to allow.</li>
<li>Provide IP address blocks in <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classless_Inter-Domain_Routing#CIDR_blocks" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">CIDR notation</a>.</li>
</ul>
<p>To completely disable external access to your database, use the <code class="language-text">--clear-ip-allow-list</code> flag:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:b"></template><!--/$-->
<p>For more options, see the <a href="/docs/cli-reference#pg-update"><code class="language-text">pg update</code> reference</a>.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-trigger-api" id="radix-_R_2bilubspiuidb_-content-api" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="api">
<p>Use the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/update-postgres/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Update Postgres instance</a> API endpoint. Provide IP address blocks in <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Classless_Inter-Domain_Routing#CIDR_blocks" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">CIDR notation</a> in the <code class="language-text">ipAllowList</code> parameter.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="connection-limits"><a href="#connection-limits" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Connection limits</h3>
<p>Your database&#x27;s maximum number of simultaneous connections depends on its compute plan&#x27;s total memory (RAM):</p>
<div class="scrollable"><table class="docs-table my-30 docs-table-simple"><thead><tr><th>Memory</th><th>Max Connections</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>
<p>&lt; 8 GB</p>
</td><td>100 connections</td></tr><tr><td>
<p>8 GB &lt;= memory &lt; 16 GB</p>
</td><td>200 connections</td></tr><tr><td>
<p>16 GB &lt;= memory &lt; 32 GB</p>
</td><td>400 connections</td></tr><tr><td>
<p>&gt;= 32 GB</p>
</td><td>500 connections</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>If you&#x27;re approaching your connection limit, consider <a href="#changing-your-compute-plan">upgrading your compute plan</a> or implementing <a href="/docs/postgresql-connection-pooling">connection pooling</a>.</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-info.svg)] border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 dark:border-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-900 dark:text-purple-100">
<p><strong>Databases on a <a href="/docs/postgresql-legacy-instance-types"><strong>legacy instance type</strong></a> support fewer connections:</strong></p>
<details>
<summary><strong>View legacy instance connection limits</strong></summary>
<div class="scrollable"><table class="docs-table my-30 docs-table-simple"><thead><tr><th>Memory</th><th>Max Connections</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>
<p>&lt;= 6 GB</p>
</td><td>97 connections</td></tr><tr><td>Between 6 GB and 10 GB</td><td>197 connections</td></tr><tr><td>
<p>&gt;= 10 GB</p>
</td><td>397 connections</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
</details>
<p>You can move your database to a flexible plan by <a href="#changing-your-compute-plan">changing its compute plan</a>.</p>
</span>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="adding-storage"><a href="#adding-storage" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Adding storage</h2>
<p>You set your database&#x27;s initial storage during <a href="#create-your-database">creation</a>. Any time after that, you can increase your database&#x27;s storage to any higher multiple of 5 GB, up to 16 TB.</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-help.svg)] border-pink-400 bg-pink-50 text-pink-900 dark:border-pink-700 dark:bg-pink-900 dark:text-pink-100">
<p><strong>Need more than 16 TB of storage?</strong></p>
<p>Please <a href="https://dashboard.render.com?contact-support" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">contact support</a> in the Render Dashboard.</p>
</span>
<p>You can increase storage <a href="#storage-autoscaling">automatically</a> or <a href="#increasing-storage-manually">manually</a>.</p>
<p><strong>Note the following:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>After you increase a database&#x27;s storage, you can&#x27;t increase it again for 6 hours.</li>
<li>It is not possible to reduce a database&#x27;s storage.</li>
<li>Databases on a <a href="/docs/postgresql-legacy-instance-types">legacy instance type</a> have a fixed storage capacity.
<ul>
<li>You can move your database to a flexible plan by <a href="#changing-your-compute-plan">changing its compute plan</a>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="storage-autoscaling"><a href="#storage-autoscaling" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Storage autoscaling</h3>
<p>You can automatically add storage to your database whenever it&#x27;s running low. With <strong>storage autoscaling</strong> enabled, Render detects when your database is 90% full and permanently increases its storage by 50%, rounded up to the nearest multiple of 5 GB.</p>
<p>Here are some example increases:</p>
<div class="scrollable"><table class="docs-table my-30 docs-table-simple"><thead><tr><th>Original Storage</th><th>New Storage</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>1 GB</td><td>5 GB</td></tr><tr><td>10 GB</td><td>15 GB</td></tr><tr><td>25 GB</td><td>40 GB</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>Enable storage autoscaling with any of the following methods:</p>
<div dir="ltr" data-orientation="horizontal" class="tab-switcher my-20" data-tab-switcher="true"><div role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="flex flex-wrap bg-gray-25 px-24 <div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3dilubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_3dilubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="dashboard"><a href="#dashboard" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Dashboard</h4>
<ol>
<li>
<p>From your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>, scroll down to the <strong>PostgreSQL Instance</strong> section and click <strong>Update</strong>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:550px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.png" width="1474" height="318" alt="Update PostgreSQL storage in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
</li>
<li>
<p>Scroll down to the <strong>Enable Storage Autoscaling</strong> field and toggle the switch.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Click <strong>Save Changes</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3dilubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_3dilubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="cli"><a href="#cli" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>CLI</h4>

<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="api"><a href="#api" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>API</h4>
<p>Use the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/update-postgres/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Update Postgres instance</a> API endpoint. Set the <code class="language-text">enableDiskAutoscaling</code> parameter to <code class="language-text">true</code>.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<p>That&#x27;s it! Render will automatically add storage to your database whenever it&#x27;s 90% full.</p>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="increasing-storage-manually"><a href="#increasing-storage-manually" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Increasing storage manually</h3>
<p>Manually add storage to your database with any of the following methods:</p>
<div dir="ltr" data-orientation="horizontal" class="tab-switcher my-20" data-tab-switcher="true"><div role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="flex flex-wrap bg-gray-25 px-24 <div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">

<ol>
<li>
<p>From your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>, scroll down to the <strong>PostgreSQL Instance</strong> section and click <strong>Update</strong>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:550px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.png" width="1474" height="318" alt="Update PostgreSQL storage in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
</li>
<li>
<p>Scroll down to the <strong>Storage</strong> field and provide a new value.</p>
<ul>
<li>Provide any multiple of 5 GB greater than the current storage capacity.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Click <strong>Save Changes</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>That&#x27;s it! The additional storage becomes available within a minute or two.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="cli-1"><a href="#cli-1" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>CLI</h4>
<p>Increase your database&#x27;s storage using the <code class="language-text">render pg update</code> command with the <code class="language-text">--disk-size-gb</code> flag:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:e"></template><!--/$-->
<p>Provide any multiple of 5 GB greater than the current storage capacity.</p>
<p>For more options, see the <a href="/docs/cli-reference#pg-update"><code class="language-text">pg update</code> reference</a>.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-trigger-api" id="radix-_R_3lilubspiuidb_-content-api" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="api">
<h4 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.25rem] font-bold relative group/header" id="api-1"><a href="#api-1" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>API</h4>
<p>Use the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/update-postgres/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Update Postgres instance</a> API endpoint. Provide the new value in the <code class="language-text">diskSizeGB</code> parameter.</p>
<p>Provide any multiple of 5 GB greater than the current storage capacity.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="running-out-of-storage"><a href="#running-out-of-storage" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Running out of storage</h3>
<p><strong>If your database exceeds its storage limit, it becomes unhealthy.</strong> Render automatically suspends the database to prevent data loss or other unexpected behavior.</p>
<p>To restore your database:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>In the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>, scroll to the bottom of your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page and click <strong>Resume Database</strong>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Wait a minute or two for the database to finish resuming.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Follow the steps to <a href="#increasing-storage-manually">manually add storage capacity</a>.</p>
<ul>
<li>If you wait too long after resuming, Render will suspend your database again. In this case, return to step 1.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p>Your database will become healthy within a few minutes.</p>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="changing-your-compute-plan"><a href="#changing-your-compute-plan" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Changing your compute plan</h2>
<p>You can change your Render Postgres database&#x27;s compute plan, which determines its available CPU and RAM. <a href="/docs/compute-plans?tab=postgres#all-plans">View available compute plans</a>.</p>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-warning.svg)] border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-800 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-200">
<p><strong>Your database will be unavailable temporarily during the change.</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>With <a href="/docs/postgresql-high-availability">high availability</a> enabled, your database is unavailable for only a few seconds.</li>
<li>Otherwise, it&#x27;s unavailable for a few minutes.</li>
</ul>
<p>Schedule your change during off hours to minimize user impact.</p>
</span>
<div dir="ltr" data-orientation="horizontal" class="tab-switcher my-20" data-tab-switcher="true"><div role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="flex flex-wrap bg-gray-25 px-24 motion-safe:transition-[height] bg-purple-600"></div></button><button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-content-cli" data-state="inactive" [--underline-height:0px] data-[state=active]:font-semibold data-[state=active]:text-black hover:data-[state=inactive]:text-black dark:text-gray-200 dark:data-[state=active]:text-white
