flex-col border-t-0 p-24 border dark:border-gray-700">
<div data-state="active" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-trigger-dashboard" id="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-content-dashboard" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="dashboard" style="animation-duration:0s">
<ol>
<li>
<p>From your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>, scroll down to the <strong>PostgreSQL Instance</strong> section and click <strong>Update</strong>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:550px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/c05028c347562ea9006e09384bff5956df1dc9186fec2f56a1033c11bb5e15b4/update-postgresql-instance.png" width="1474" height="318" alt="Update Postgres compute plan in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
</li>
<li>
<p>Under <strong>Plan Options</strong>, select a new <strong>Compute Plan</strong>.</p>
<ul>
<li>If your database currently uses a <a href="/docs/postgresql-legacy-instance-types">legacy instance type</a>, you won&#x27;t be able to move <em>back</em> to a legacy instance type after changing.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>Click <strong>Save Changes</strong>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>That&#x27;s it! Your new instance will be available within a few minutes.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-trigger-cli" id="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="cli">
<p>Change your database&#x27;s compute plan using the <code class="language-text">render pg update</code> command with the <code class="language-text">--plan</code> flag:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:f"></template><!--/$-->
<p>If your database currently uses a <a href="/docs/postgresql-legacy-instance-types">legacy instance type</a>, you won&#x27;t be able to move back to a legacy instance type after changing.</p>
<p>For more options, see the <a href="/docs/cli-reference#pg-update"><code class="language-text">pg update</code> reference</a>.</p>
</div>
<div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-trigger-api" id="radix-_R_47ilubspiuidb_-content-api" tabindex="0" class="switcher-tab data-[state=inactive]:hidden first:[&amp;&gt;*]:mt-0 last:[&amp;&gt;*]:mb-0" data-tab-switcher-item="true" data-tab-value="api">
<p>Use the <a href="https://api-docs.render.com/reference/update-postgres/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Update Postgres instance</a> API endpoint. Provide the new plan in the <code class="language-text">plan</code> parameter.</p>
<p>If your database currently uses a <a href="/docs/postgresql-legacy-instance-types">legacy instance type</a>, you won&#x27;t be able to move back to a legacy instance type after changing.</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="adding-multiple-databases-to-a-single-instance"><a href="#adding-multiple-databases-to-a-single-instance" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Adding multiple databases to a single instance</h2>
<p>You can create additional databases in your Render Postgres instance with the following steps:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>In your terminal, open a psql session to your instance using the <strong>PSQL Command</strong> provided in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:600px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/f0ccbf4aaa8a57f18fb0c4b8095d9929783b72f6276221a708de27f1563b5fbf/postgresql-external-connections.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/f0ccbf4aaa8a57f18fb0c4b8095d9929783b72f6276221a708de27f1563b5fbf/postgresql-external-connections.png" width="1320" height="310" alt="Postgres public connection details in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
</li>
<li>
<p>Run <code class="language-text">CREATE DATABASE &lt;name&gt;</code>, providing the name for your new database.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>You&#x27;re all set! Use your instance&#x27;s same internal and external URLs to connect, except substitute your new database&#x27;s name as the final component:</p>
<!--$?--><template id="B:10"></template><!--/$-->
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="encryption"><a href="#encryption" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Encryption</h2>
<p>Render Postgres databases are encrypted at rest using AES-256 data encryption. This encryption applies to both primary and replica instances, along with all backups. <a href="#external-connections">External connections</a> to your database are encrypted in transit using Render-managed TLS certificates.</p>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="metrics-and-logs"><a href="#metrics-and-logs" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Metrics and logs</h2>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="dashboard-2"><a href="#dashboard-2" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Dashboard</h3>
<p>View a variety of metrics for your database (disk usage, active connections, etc.) from its <strong>Metrics</strong> page in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>:</p>
<span class="block" style="max-width:600px">
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/2496f8ea803fbca8a35ffd7b161707dc4b668396926249093715da6f850b998e/postgresql-metrics.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/2496f8ea803fbca8a35ffd7b161707dc4b668396926249093715da6f850b998e/postgresql-metrics.png" width="1780" height="990" alt="Postgres metrics in the Render Dashboard"/></picture></p>
</span>
<p>For details, see <a href="/docs/service-metrics#available-metrics">Service Metrics</a>.</p>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="datadog"><a href="#datadog" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Datadog</h3>
<p>The Datadog integration provides additional metrics related to your PostgreSQL instance&#x27;s host and disk. You can also use the Datadog UI to create dashboards and alerts for your database.</p>
<p>For details, see the <a href="/docs/datadog#setting-up-postgres-monitoring">Datadog integration docs</a>.</p>
<h3 class="typography scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-10 mt-40 text-[1.75rem] leading-snug font-montreal font-medium relative group/header" id="viewing-slow-query-logs"><a href="#viewing-slow-query-logs" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Viewing slow query logs</h3>
<p>Queries that take longer than 2 seconds are logged with a line that starts with <code class="language-text">duration:</code> followed by the SQL statement. Here&#x27;s an example:</p>
<p><picture><source srcSet="/docs-assets/60637469aa0d6f93e61c05f3269e5f5da5cfd1ac4ea345092556ac0a6c5b91f7/slow-query-log-example.webp" type="image/webp"/><img src="/docs-assets/60637469aa0d6f93e61c05f3269e5f5da5cfd1ac4ea345092556ac0a6c5b91f7/slow-query-log-example.png" width="1274" height="282" alt="Postgres slow query log"/></picture></p>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="deleting-your-database"><a href="#deleting-your-database" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Deleting your database</h2>
<span class="callout-block my-20 block border-1 p-[17px] pl-60 [&amp;&gt;*:first-child]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;*:last-child]:mb-0 bg-[17px_17px] bg-no-repeat [background-size:24px] bg-[url(/images/icon-warning.svg)] border-yellow-300 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-800 dark:border-yellow-700 dark:bg-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-200">
<p><strong>Render does not retain backups or snapshots of a deleted database instance!</strong></p>
<p>Make sure to download any necessary backups before deleting your database.</p>
</span>
<p>You can delete a database instance in the <a href="https://dashboard.render.com" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Render Dashboard</a>. Scroll down to the bottom of your database&#x27;s <strong>Info</strong> page and click <strong>Delete Database</strong>.</p>
<h2 class="typography text-heading-md font-roobert scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin,120px)] lg:scroll-mt-[var(--docs-heading-scroll-margin-lg,90px)] mb-30 mt-60 relative group/header" id="additional-topics"><a href="#additional-topics" class="not-prose absolute top-0 -left-24 hidden w-28 items-center opacity-0 outline-none transition-opacity focus:opacity-100 group-hover/header:opacity-100 md:flex h-[1em] h-[1lh]"><svg aria-hidden="true" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a>Additional topics</h2>
